Title: Threnos To iest Lament iedyney s. Powszechney Apostolskiey Wschodniey Cerkwie, z obiasnieniem Dogmat Wiary: Pierwej z Greckiego na Slowienski, a teraz z Slowienskiego na Polski przelozony (Threnos, that is the Lament of the one holy universal apostolic Eastern Church)
Creator: Meletij Smotryc'kyj (Meletius Smotrzyski), who signs on the title page as 'Theophilus Orthologus'; the host catalogue gives the name in square brackets. Dedicated to Michal Wisniowiecki
Publisher: Vilnius (the colophon reads 'w Wilnie', the year digits garbled in the OCR); printer not read
Publication Date: 1610 (host catalogue date; the colophon year is garbled in the OCR)
Rights: Public Domain (a 1610 print; the host record states 'Domena publiczna (public domain)')
Language: pol
Source: Internet Archive item jbc.bj.uj.edu.pl.NDIGSTDR013226 (Jagiellonian Digital Library, collection polishpublicdomain), file NDIGSTDR013226_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: the item record states 'Domena publiczna (public domain)'
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from this header and the long-s normalisation (ſ to s, 0 replaced). Nothing else was corrected, re-spaced or retyped. Rights basis: a print of 1610, centuries older than the 95-year rule, clears the public-domain-by-date rule; the host record states public domain.
Content note: SECOND SOURCE WITNESS FOR QUOTING: the OCR of the 17th-century black-letter print is badly garbled (about 94,000 tokens, many of them broken), with a page of scan noise at the head, so quotes need the page images (the item carries the PDF and page images). This is the Polish text of the Threnos, the Orthodox church's lament against the union and the Latin church, said on the title page to be put into Polish from the Slavonic, which was put from the Greek. The Orthodox opening shot of the print war that Brest began. OCR sampled at five points (10/30/50/70/90 percent): words recognisable but with many letter errors throughout.
